## PolarSurfaceHodgeIndex (mathlib only)
- PolarSurface E: symmetric pairing, isotropic H,V with H·V=1 (fibres on C×C; pole directions e^{±u/2} on scaling surface of ℤ, pairing with test = transform at ∓i/2).
- polar(x,y) = (x·H)(y·V)+(x·V)(y·H); defect δ = polar − pair (CASTELNUOVO–SEVERI DEFECT); prim x° = x − (x·V)H − (x·H)V; pair_prim_prim (x°·y° = −δ); pair_eq_polar_add_prim (orthogonal decomposition); defect_prim (defect sees only primitive part).
- HodgeIndex (neg semidef on primitive); CastelnuovoSeveri (x² ≤ 2(x·H)(x·V)); ample H+V, (H+V)²=2; AmpleHodgeIndex; hodgeIndex_tfae (HI ⇔ CS ⇔ δ(x,x) ≥ 0 (= WEIL POSITIVITY with tests + explicit formula) ⇔ ample HI).
- defect_sq_le_of_hodgeIndex (δ(x,y)² ≤ δ(x,x)δ(y,y): THE ONE INEQUALITY OF WEIL'S PROOF); hodgeIndex_iff_no_positive_plane (single positive square).

Cauchy–Schwarz posSlack bound b·posSlack ≥ (trQ + trQ₋ − cb)². Displaced pair (‖x‖² = 1+η, ‖y‖² = η): eigenvalues 2(1+η), −2η; three thresholds at κ = 4/3: s₁ ≥ 3/4 iff (1+2η)² ≥ 4/3 (η ≥ 0.0774); totalSlack ≥ N/12 iff η ≥ 1/6; off-line slack ≥ N/12 iff (1+2η)² ≥ 2 (η ≥ 0.2071). Explicit counterexample s₁=179, p=32, η=1/16, N=243: κ=4/3, non-coalesced (Q not PSD), s₁ < 3/4 N, totalSlack < N/12 (abstract).

Lorentz–polar identity 2B A = 2evenPoleMoment² − 2oddPoleMoment²; weilPair F F = fullZeroForm F; odd: poleCharge = 2S² = 2A² = −2BA (negative self-intersection of polar projection A(H−V)). ## TheLambdaRingOfTheScalingSurface (imports BezoutEffectivity, TheScalingSurface, HeckeIncidenceCartesianLaw)

## ArithmeticSectionSpace (imports SSHA.TheStabilityLawAtTheSpacingScale, SourceDeterminantalSections)
- Doc: arithmetic section Σ_{n≤N} g_n(u − log n) with independent profiles g_n supported in [0,w]; below integer-log spacing prime lags read exactly the Hecke incidence pairing of profiles; mixed archimedean correlations controlled by separate masses; for explicit arithmeticSectionRatio, width w ≤ ratio·log(1+1/N) ⇒ markovEnergy ≥ square mass. Kernel of sinh pole moment is nonzero source-defined subspace; its L² source cone has RR + Ample on original scaling subsurface, no spectral assumptions. Support restriction is part of theorem (NOT HI on all tests).

- markovEnergy_arithmeticSection_ge (markovEnergy ≥ (2γ'(0) + log(1/w) − log N − spacingConstant)∫f² for w ≤ spacingRatio·atomSpacing N: Lévy log(1/w) + tilted prime rows; crossRow_le_saving pays archimedean cross kernel per row); arithmeticSectionRatio = spacingWidthRatio·e^{−1}; _pos; _le; arithmeticSectionGain_ge_one; integral_sq_le_markovEnergy_arithmeticSection (UNCONDITIONAL: ∫f² ≤ markovEnergy, uniform in N and profiles); arithmeticSectionTests N w (submodule of sections); sectionSinhMoment (linear sinhHalfMoment); sinhNeutralArithmeticSections = sections ∩ ker sinh; integral_sq_le_weilPair_sinhNeutralArithmeticSection (W = markov + 2cosh² − 2sinh² ⇒ ∫f² ≤ W when sinh = 0); sinhNeutralArithmeticSections_effectivity (RR, Ample, HI on that subsurface UNCONDITIONAL); mem_arithmeticSectionTests_of_support; exists_nonzero_sinhNeutralArithmeticSection (two disjoint bumps combined to kill sinh moment).

- Doc: tilted prime rows retain diagonal reserve with weights 1 + (n/N)(1 + log N); these bound both pole moments by complementary sums; weighted Young pays the adverse pole product ⇒ removes sinh-neutrality.

Widths shrink with N: this is a SHORT-SUPPORT positivity, not RH.

## SourceConeBoundaryRigidity (imports AllApertureSourceRiemannRochAndCoercivityLaws)
- Doc: for nonneg fibre-invariant source energy, RR requires Φ ≤ defect; Bézout requires reverse (translate source to boundary polar = Φ then test self-intersection) ⇒ BOTH axioms ⇔ Φ = defect; then source cone = closed positive cone. Fibre degrees don't give Bézout; strict cone has same obstruction.

- KEY NO-GO: any source cone with RR and Bézout must have source energy = defect; a coercive L² floor gives RR but never Bézout unless W is exactly scalar.

- Doc: every finite-dimensional linear reading leaves nonzero real test in every positive interval; joint with low-frequency grid and pole ⇒ near-radical concentration + grid estimate contradict any upper bound ⇒ W unbounded above (Rayleigh) at every aperture ⇒ no finite scalar L² reserve has Bézout; arbitrary postprocessing of finite-dimensional reading fails Bézout.

## OnePrimeAgainstTwoAndWolffTowerObstruction (imports OnePrimeSeesEveryWellFoundedLevel, TheTowerDefectCountsTheFrobeniusEigenvalues, HTGeometry.PositiveWolffMeasure, RHTower.WeilLagSingleBaseWolffConfiguration)
- File doc ("Open Problem 7 — one prime against two"): single-prime tower Gram towerGram Z g ℓ K is Toeplitz, entry (j,k) = Laurent moment towerMoment Z g ℓ (j−k) = Σ' τ X_τ(g) conj(X_{1−τ̄}(g)) β(τ)^{j−k}; depends on (Z,g) only via Laurent moments. OBSTRUCTION: two disjoint outer annuli, difference of normalized positive Wolff measures gives injective family with ALL two-sided Laurent moments 0 (incl. m=0) but nonzero class sums — top-down class extraction fails when moduli accumulate at a supremum from below (sharpness of tsum_ite_eq_zero_of_moments_of_wellFounded). Single-prime Toeplitz blindness: symmetric Wolff configuration in strip 1/2 < Re < 1/2+Θ, real parts increasing to 1/2+Θ (non-well-founded), locally finite ordinates, infinitely many outer Frobenius eigenvalues, all nonzero moments vanish, m=0 moment 4Σc_i > 0 ⇒ Toeplitz = positive scalar, hermNegIndex 0 on every window (one prime BLIND). Positive survivors: bounded-ordinate levels are well-founded (finite_window); non-well-founded ⇒ unbounded ordinates; wfVisibleSet; above-threshold extraction; two-prime rescue.

- File doc: Q_γ(F₀) = Q(F₀cos γ·) + Q(F₀ sin γ·); positivity of Q_γ at every height and aperture is RH (TRAP). OFF-LINE FIELD Φ(γ) = Σ_q (Re q − 1/2)²/(1 + (Im q − γ)²)² (offLineField), ≡ 0 under RH. Pointwise law (neg_offLineField_le_fullZeroForm): ∀A ≥ 0, F₀ ∈ C²_c([−A, A]), γ: Q_γ(F₀) ≥ −4(A+1)²e^A N(F₀)² Φ(γ) — negative part read by ONE function of height alone. Depth difference ≤ 4(A+1)|δ|e^{A/2}N/(1 + x²) (factor δ from conjugate-point difference, decay from two IBPs). Short windows: |{γ ∈ [Γ, Γ+H]: Φ(γ) > η}| ≤ CH/(η log Γ) (squared depths near window sum to O(H/log Γ) by Selberg short-window near-line density). Selection: outside measure CH/(η log Γ), every height satisfies Q_γ(F₀) ≥ −4η(A+1)²e^A N² for ALL apertures and tests simultaneously; exceptional set independent of aperture and test.

## UpperLefschetzBoundsAreBlindOnlyToRealZeros (imports TheFirstNegativeLagOfACurve, WhichArithmeticDataForceTheSign)
- File doc: Bombieri needs an UPPER point-count bound. On curve datum: LOWER bound N_k ≥ q^k + 1 − Cq^{k/2} (trace_k ≤ C) forces unitarity; UPPER bound N_k ≤ q^k + 1 + Cq^{k/2} (trace_k ≥ −C) forces unitarity UNLESS some outer eigenvalue is a positive real (real zero right of line). One statement on exponential sums: top unimodular block w_i: window mean of Re Σw_i^k = #{w_i = 1}, mean square ≥ #J/2 ⇒ Re Σ w_i^k > 1/8 infinitely often, and < −1/8 infinitely often when no w_i = 1. Golden fake curve satisfies upper bound with C = 0 (outer eigenvalue φ real). On scaling surface: difference lag tests g − g(·−n log q) all Weil-nonneg ⇔ pure; sum tests g + g(· − n log q) detect every impure curve without real outer eigenvalue; all nonneg on golden curve.

## TheDefectOfAGraphIsTwiceItsDegree (imports UpperLefschetzBoundsAreBlindOnlyToRealZeros, TheLambdaRingOfTheScalingSurface, SourceRiemannRochAwayFromTheLowFrequencyDivisor)
- File doc: on a polar surface, x·V = 1 and x² = 0 ⇒ defect(x,x) = 2(x·H) (LINEAR reading). Over F_q genus one: graphs of endomorphisms m − nF; graphCycle m n has x·V = 1, x·H = deg(m − nF) = m² − tmn + qn² (t = q + 1 − N₁), span defect 2g(m² + qn²) − 2tmn; genus one: isotropic (adjunction) and defect = 2 deg. Hodge index on window = degree positivity of ℤ[F] = HASSE's proof: unitary ⇔ deg(m − nF) ≥ 0 ∀ integers ⇔ t² ≤ 4q. Golden: t = 5, deg(2 − F) = −1. Hasse uses that ℤ[F] is a RING of correspondences (graph map quadratic: theorem of the square). Scaling surface of ℤ has Hecke graphs of the multiplicative MONOID only: distinct Hecke scales numerically free ⇒ no parallelogram relation even up to fibre classes. KEY STRUCTURAL GAP.

## HarmonicCouplingForcesSourceBlindness (imports MaximalBlindIdealOfTheMeyerSource, HTGeometry.{CosinePolynomialPurchaseCap, FrullaniIntegral, RiemannZetaMeromorphic}, RHTower.{VonMangoldtThreeFourOneSeries, ZetaResidueFactorDerivative, ZetaLogDerivResidue, ZetaZeroFreeRegion, WeilFullConeRiemannKernelRigidity})
- File doc: single-channel correspondence-stable positivity is exhausted by the blind ideal; escape requires coupling DILATED HARMONIC channels via the multiplicative group law n^{−ikω} = (n^{−iω})^k (Mertens 3-4-1 mechanism). (1) degree ladder: K = 1 forces a₁ ≤ a₀ (cannot weight aligned zero above simple pole); Fejér autocorrelation (1,2,1) gives (6, 8, 2) crossing a₀ < a₁ at minimal degree M = 2; universal STRICT Fejér barrier a₁ < 2a₀ for every nonnegative cosine polynomial. (2) all-aperture coupled ceiling: modulating nonnegative f along ±mω makes coupled prime sum AND coupled critical-line sum ≥ 0 ⇒ off-line coupled zero sum ≤ pole + archimedean terms (no narrow-support restriction). (3) quantized divisor-order forcing: harmonic positivity on σ > 1, a₀ < a₁, simple pole μ(0) ≤ 1, harmonic pole isolation μ(kt) = 0, integer divisor ⇒ μ_zero(t) = 0; on ζ excludes boundary zeros, with derivative growth excludes ξ zeros from both reflected dVP collars. - File doc: if Σ_{a∈A}|a|^{−s} < ∞ for some s < 1, there is a compactly supported test whose transform vanishes on the infinite set A and at no prescribed finite set F of non-real points. Ingham bump: iterated convolution of boxes 1_{[−ε_n,ε_n]}/(2ε_n), ε_n = (n+1)^{−p}, p > 1 → smooth φ on [−Σε, Σε], Φ(z) = Π sin(ε_n z)/(ε_n z); on ℝ |Φ(x)| ≤ e^{2 − (|x|/e)^{1/p}}; Φ ≠ 0 off ℝ. Annihilator: L_a h = h + (ia)^{−1}h' multiplies transform by (1 − z/a); |Π(1 − x/a_k)| ≤ e^{|x|^s Σ|a_k|^{−s}/s}, 1/p > s ⇒ uniform convergence to C² test g with ĝ = Π(1 − z/a_k)·Φ.

## WolffBlindnessRequiresSeparation (imports OnePrimeSeesEveryWellFoundedLevel)
- File doc: classes extracted by COMPLEX ANALYSIS instead of top-down Cesàro: discrete Cauchy transform G(z) = Σ q_τ/(1 − c_τ z) holomorphic off closure of poles c_τ^{−1}; vanishing moments ⇒ G = 0 near 0 ⇒ G = 0 on every connected open set through 0 avoiding poles; residue at an accessible pole = class sum ⇒ 0. Towers: poles = reciprocal eigenvalues of active zeros and conjugates (activeFoldedSpectrum); every visible b with b^{−1} ACCESSIBLE from origin off its closure is seen; finitely many by one window. NO order condition on real parts. BLINDNESS IS SEPARATION: if every window PSD, every visible b^{−1} is walled off from origin by the closed folded spectrum (Wolff needs folded zeros to SEPARATE, not merely accumulate). Countable folded spectra in unit disc: every b with b^{−1} isolated is seen; real parts increasing to sup blind a fixed test only if folded points fill a continuum around the origin.

NON-ARITHMETIC (ℓ/log q irrational): vertical translates have distinct eigenvalues ⇒ infinite defect. Unitarity: one probe's tower PSD at every scale ⇔ |β_i| = 1 ∀i. ## MomentBoundsCapTheOffLineDepth (imports Mathlib only; namespace ...MomentDepth)
- File doc: L-polynomial L(u) = ∏(1 − αu); zeros 1/α inside |u| < r ⇔ deep |α|r > 1. JENSEN CEILING: ∏_{deep}|α|r ≤ max_{|u|=r}|L(u)| (reflect deep factors across circle — keeps |L| on circle, removes zeros — then maximum principle at 0). Moments: weighted 2k-th moment bound M over finite family ⇒ depth ≤ (M/w)^{1/(2k)}. SHARPNESS: inserting off-line pair of depths ρ > 1, ρ^{−1} multiplies depth product by ρ and circle max by ≤ (1+ρ)(1+ρ^{−1}). INSIGHT/TRAP: moment information certifies zero location ONLY through depth product; a degree-independent factor hides any fixed number of off-line pairs ⇒ generating functions of moments (finite, affine, Kac–Moody multiple Dirichlet series) read exponential growth in degree, so their domains of holomorphy/natural boundaries CANNOT detect such pairs.
